<script setup lang="ts">
import { useRoute } from "vitepress";
import { onMounted, watch } from "vue";

const route = useRoute();

/** `#v7-2-0` opens that release card, from this page or any other. */
function openFromHash(hash: string) {
	const target = document.getElementById(hash.replace(/^#/, ""));
	if (target instanceof HTMLDetailsElement) target.open = true;
}

onMounted(() => openFromHash(route.hash));
watch(() => route.hash, openFromHash);
</script>

# What's news! - Changelog

Every release KatanaKit JS has shipped, newest first — open a card to see the changes behind it. The newest one is already open, and the raw file lives in [`CHANGELOG.md`](https://github.com/senseikatana/katanakit-js/blob/main/CHANGELOG.md). Upgrading across majors? Start at the [upgrade guides](/guides/upgrade-to/).

<details class="changelog-release" id="v6-5-1">
<summary><span class="changelog-release__version">v6.5.1</span><span class="changelog-release__date">Oct 4, 2026</span><span class="changelog-release__count">3 changes</span></summary>

<div class="changelog-release__body">

#### Security

- 🔒 **Secret management hardening** — removed real Cloudflare API tokens from `.env` (`CLOUDFLARE_API_TOKEN`, `CLOUDFLARE_ZONE_SECURITY_TOKEN` commented out); added "Secret Management" section to `AGENTS.md` and `CONTRIBUTING.md` documenting best practices for handling secrets (empty local `.env`, `.env.example` template only, CI secrets for real values).

#### Changed

- ✨ **TypeScript strictness** — enabled `isolatedModules: true` in `tsconfig.json` for stricter transpilation checks (required by esbuild/SWC and future bundler compatibility).
- 📚 **Removed `.github/` directory** — CI release workflows that kept failing with `Deploy failed` were untracked; docs deploy locally with `bun run cf:deploy`.

</div>

</details>
